# Ledger: Hertz Global Holdings, Inc., FY2009 to Q4 FY2013

```
Step:        F5, The ledger
Company:     Hertz Global Holdings, Inc. (SEC CIK 1364479), NYSE: HTZ [P0, header table]
Framework:   US GAAP [P0, header table; CS section 1]
Currency:    USD [P0]
Units:       USD millions throughout this output unless stated. P1 statements.xlsx is in USD millions; the FY2011
             and FY2012 10-K text statements are in USD thousands and were divided by 1,000 in code (C4);
             the FY2013 10-K and all earnings-release tables are in USD millions [P0, header table]
Fiscal year: ends 31 December [P0]
Ledgers:     L1 ledger-F1.csv 7 rows; L2 ledger-F2.csv 13 rows; L3 ledger-F3.csv 18 rows; L4 ledger-F4.csv 33 rows
             (71 rows); CS context-sheet.md (26 events, CS E1 to CS E26) [calc, C1]
Also used:   earnings releases P269 to P296; proxies P297 (fiscal 2011 pay) and P298 (fiscal 2012 pay); P1; 10-K notes
Not attached: consensus.csv
```

Marks. `[P#, location]` is a pack file. `[L1]` to `[L4]` with a row ID, and `[CS, item]`, are the attached step files. `[calc]` figures come from the code blocks C1 to C6 in the Code section. The context sheet numbers its events CS E1 to CS E26; this step's checklist rows are written "checklist E1" to "checklist E6" to keep the two apart.

## 1. Summary

- **Threads:** 25 in total: P1 3, P2 7, P3 12, P4 3 [calc, C5].
- **Rows merged:** 79: 71 from L1 to L4 and 8 new context rows from this step (checklist E2 three, E3 three, E6 two) [calc, C5]. Row priorities: P1 8, P2 6, P3 47, P4 18 [calc, C5].
- **Rows excluded as malformed:** 0. Every input row has all eleven columns, a unique ID, a locator, both readings and an empty Verdict [calc, C1].
- **Context rows run:** checklist E2 (guidance), E3 (compensation), E4 (turnover and controls, no new row: it is carried by F1-E4-1 and F1-E4-2), E6 (differential disclosure). **Not run:** checklist E1 (no consensus.csv) and E5 (no peer data). See "Could not run".
- **Where the weight sits.** Three P1 threads. T1 (fleet depreciation and residual values, FY2013) joins 14 rows from four steps, six of them P1 [calc, C5]. T2 (adjusted measures) now also covers pay: the annual bonus and the performance stock units are set on adjusted pre-tax income and Corporate EBITDA [P297 p.38; P298 p.33]. The new context rows add two things the single-statement steps could not see. FY2013 results fell below the low end of the guidance range lowered on 26 Sep 2013, on all five metrics [calc, C2]. The FY2011 performance-unit payout rested on Corporate EBITDA 0.5 above target, on figures the company later revised [calc, C3; P297 p.45].
- Guidance and consensus comparisons describe expectations, not the company's accounts. Rows F5-E2-01 to F5-E2-03 and F5-E3-03 are labelled that way.

## 2. Reading plan

In rank order. Each line: what to read, where, and a question either reading of the thread could answer.

1. **T1 (P1), FY2013 fleet depreciation rate reductions, lengthened holding periods and residual-value statements alongside disposal losses.** Read Note 8, P74 (AR-FY2013_notes-08-depreciation-of-revenue-earning-equipment-and.txt) p.126, then the MD&A U.S. car rental discussion, P62 (AR-FY2013_mdna.txt) pp.42 to 43 and 54, the FY2013 outlook in P285 (releases/ER-Q4-FY2012.txt) p.5 line 122, and the residual sensitivity on the Q4 slides, P296 p.21. Question: were the 2013 U.S. rate reductions (H1 -17.3, Q3 -18.0, Q4 -8.9 [L3 F3-C8-01]) each set on residual evidence available when they were made, so that the later disposal losses (Q4 -27.7 [L3 F3-C8-01]) came from residuals that weakened afterwards; or did rates keep falling after the company's own documents described weaker residuals ("Auction/dealer residuals softer than expected", P292 p.11 [L4 F4-D3-04])?
2. **T2 (P1), Recurring charges excluded from adjusted measures, label and category changes, and the same measures driving incentive pay.** Read the FY2013 segment reconciliation with its footnotes, P77 (AR-FY2013_notes-11-segment-information.txt) pp.134 to 135, the Corporate EBITDA table in P295 (releases/ER-Q4-FY2013.txt) p.2 lines 603 to 652, and the pay sections of P298 (proxy/PROXY-2013.txt) pp.28 to 34. Question: does each excluded category (restructuring, integration, relocation, impairment and other, "Other") tie to a programme with a stated end that has not recurred, or does the same category recur each year while being excluded from the measures that set annual and long-term pay?
3. **T3 (P1), Finite-lived intangible and other asset lives stated in two ranges; soft-asset share at series high.** Read Note 2, P68 (AR-FY2013_notes-02-summary-of-significant-accounting-policies.txt) p.91 (useful-life table) and p.93 (intangibles paragraph), then Note 3, P69 (AR-FY2013_notes-03-goodwill-and-other-intangible-assets.txt). Question: which range, "3 to 10 years" or "two to fifteen years", governs amortization of finite-lived intangibles, and does FY2013 amortization of 121.5 [P1, IS_A] fit the carrying amounts in Note 3 at that range?
4. **T4 (P2), FY2013 revision of FY2011 to Q3 2013 for out-of-period errors, its totals, comparatives and effect on pay measures.** Read the revision tables in Note 2, P68 pp.83 to 89, the note in P295 line 260 and the quarterly data, P84 (AR-FY2013_notes-18-quarterly-financial-information-unaudited.txt) p.149. Question: is the 1.6 between the release total (47.9) and the 10-K total (46.3) a fourth-quarter error or a drafting figure, and do the revised FY2011 and FY2012 lines show the vendor-incentive correction moving the pay measures (API, Corporate EBITDA) by the pre-tax amount, as the FY2012 Corporate EBITDA did (-9.2) [calc, C4]?
5. **T5 (P2), FY2013 receivables decline not traced to lines, bad-debt provision above Schedule II, and Simply Wheelz exposure below the stated range.** Read the Simply Wheelz text in Note 4, P70 (AR-FY2013_notes-04-business-combinations-and-divestitures.txt) pp.100 to 101, Schedule II, P86 (AR-FY2013_schedule-ii.txt) p.158, and the FY2013 cash flow statement, P66 (AR-FY2013_financial-statements.txt) p.80. Question: is the 4.0 by which the cash-flow provision (45.9) exceeds Schedule II charged to expense (41.9) [L2 F2-A3-02] a provision against Simply Wheelz amounts outside the trade allowance, and where does the part of the Q3 exposure range (50 to 70, P265 p.37) above the 44.0 charge (P77 p.134) sit [L4 F4-C6-02]?
6. **T6 (P2), FY2013 internal control conclusion in the year of the revision, a note requirement not met at year end, an ERP change and a CFO change; audit report and consent.** Read Item 9A, P87 (AR-FY2013_controls.txt), the debt note on the HVF II Series 2013-B notes, P71 (AR-FY2013_notes-05-debt.txt) p.108, and the subsequent events note, P85 p.150. Question: what was the requirement that was "unknowingly not met", and does Item 9A or the audit report (P65 p.74) say how the CS E24 errors and that lapse were evaluated as control deficiencies, or is the conclusion stated without that evaluation?
7. **T7 (P2), Cash-flow classification revisions and cash from operations relative to income.** Read the FY2013 cash flow statement, P66 pp.80 to 81, and the cash flow revision table in Note 2, P68 p.84. Question: which FY2012 lines moved between capital expenditures and disposal proceeds (15.7) and among receivables, payables and accruals [L1 F1-C15-4], and do those moves change cash from operations relative to income in the P1 CF_A series [L3 F3-C17-01]?
8. **T8 (P2), FY2013 public liability and property damage reserve at its lowest share of revenue, with reworded reserve basis.** Read the reserve estimate in critical estimates, P63 (AR-FY2013_critical-estimates.txt) p.45, the reserve sentence in P61 (AR-FY2013_legal.txt) p.138 and Note 2, P68 p.92. Question: did the basis wording that adds "rental volume" from FY2012 change how the reserve was measured, and is any prior-year development given that would test "relatively minor adjustments" against a reserve at 3.23% of revenue, its lowest in FY2009 to FY2013 [L2 F2-A16-02]?
9. **T9 (P2), FY2013 accrued liabilities at their lowest share of revenue with less line detail; cash flow credited to payment timing.** Read the FY2013 balance sheet, P66 p.75, the operating cash flow paragraph in P62 p.64, and the revision footnotes in P68 p.87. Question: what makes up the 283.5 by which FY2013 accrued liabilities sit below the FY2012 revised intensity [L2 F2-A16-01], and does "the timing of our payments" refer to accrued liabilities, trade payables or something else?
10. **T10 (P2), 2012 restructuring rollforward reprinted in FY2013 with 10.0 moved from other reductions to cash payments.** Read both 2012 restructuring rollforwards, P50 (AR-FY2012_notes-13-restructuring.txt) p.126 and P80 (AR-FY2013_notes-14-restructuring.txt) p.141. Question: was the 10.0 now shown as 2012 cash payments paid in cash in 2012, or was it the facility-closure reserve reduction the FY2012 10-K described, and does either filing explain the change?
11. **T11 (P3), FY2013 contingencies: Sobel potential judgment without stated accrual, new securities class action, class-action accruals, SEC investigation closed.** Read the contingencies text, P61 pp.136 to 138. Question: for Sobel, is there an accrual or a reasonably possible range against the stated potential judgment above 40.0 (6.03% of FY2013 pre-tax income [L4 F4-C10-01]), and what does the company say about estimating loss in the Ramirez class action?
12. **T12 (P3), Headquarters relocation: new risk factor and company purchases of executives' homes.** Read the related-party note, P82 (AR-FY2013_notes-16-related-party-transactions.txt) p.147, and the Q3 2013 note, P262 p.34. Question: were homes bought for 2.5 in Q4 2013 [L4 F4-D4-01] at averaged appraisals under the standard programme, and are resale results or losses recorded anywhere?
13. **T13 (P3), FY2013 risk factors drop the statement that no long-term car supply arrangements exist.** Read Item 1, P59 (AR-FY2013_business.txt), fleet acquisition section. Question: does the FY2013 10-K describe any long-term car supply arrangement, or was the FY2012 sentence dropped with no change in the arrangements?
14. **T14 (P3), Multiemployer pension risk factor wording changed FY2011 to FY2013.** Read the multiemployer zone table in Note 6, P72 (AR-FY2013_notes-06-employee-retirement-benefits.txt) p.121. Question: do the remaining plans' funding zones support the FY2013 wording change from a statement of underfunding to a possibility?
15. **T15 (P3), Inventory shown as one line with no cost method or reserve disclosed, FY2011 to FY2013.** Read the inventories line and policy, P66 p.75 and P68 p.90. Question: is a cost-flow method or write-down disclosed anywhere, and is inventory (0.38% of assets [L2 F2-A11-01]) small enough that its absence is immaterial?
16. **T16 (P3), Interest shown net of amounts capitalized with no amount disclosed, FY2011 to FY2013.** Read the cash flow supplemental line, P66 p.81, and the interest paragraph in P62 p.66. Question: is any amount of capitalized interest disclosed, or is the caption standard wording with nil capitalized?
17. **T17 (P3), Securitization references are fleet financing; no receivable sale found, FY2011 to FY2013.** Read the fleet financing descriptions in P71 pp.112 to 114. Question: are any receivables sold or derecognized under the fleet facilities, or only pledged as collateral?
18. **T18 (P3), FY2011 and FY2012 guidance raised during the year with results at or above the top; pay targets near the low end of initial guidance.** Read the target-setting text in P297 (proxy/PROXY-2012.txt) pp.38 to 40 and P298 pp.28 to 29, and the outlooks in P269 p.5 and P277 p.5. Question: were pay thresholds and targets set from the same business plan as the public guidance, and does anything show the plan or guidance being revised after the targets were fixed?
19. **T19 (P3), Like-kind exchange risk factor adds fleet net book value as a trigger, FY2012.** Read Note 9, P75 (AR-FY2013_notes-09-taxes-on-income.txt). Question: how much deferred tax relates to the like-kind exchange programme, and would a fall in fleet net book value trigger a payment?
20. **T20 (P3), Gains on sales of operations to franchisees included in operating income, policy text added FY2012.** Read the franchise policy in P39 (AR-FY2012_notes-02-summary-of-significant-accounting-policies.txt) p.84 and P68 p.90. Question: what gains on sales of operations to franchisees were included in operating income each year?
21. **T21 (P3), Stock-option volatility method changed in FY2012.** Read Note 7 on stock-based compensation, P44 (AR-FY2012_notes-07-stock-based-compensation.txt). Question: what effect did the move from the calculated value method to historical volatility have on 2012 option expense?
22. **T22 (P3), Q3 2011 revision of FY2008 to FY2010 described in different terms and amounts across the 10-Q, the 10-K and the Q4 FY2011 release.** Read the Q3 2011 revision note, P131 (Q3-FY2011_notes-02-basis-of-presentation-and-recently-issued.txt) pp.7 to 8, and the FY2011 10-K revision note, P11 (AR-FY2011_notes-02-summary-of-significant-accounting-policies.txt) p.92. Question: do the (26.9) to 31 Dec 2007 retained earnings and (8.7) to 31 Dec 2008 accumulated deficit describe the same corrections at different dates?
23. **T23 (P4), FY2013 sales growth index highest in series.** Read the FY2013 release opening, P295 p.1, and the Dollar Thrifty note, P70 pp.97 to 99. Question: how much of FY2013 revenue growth (19.4% of FY2012 revenue [L3 F3-B4-02]) is the first full year of Dollar Thrifty?
24. **T24 (P4), Q2 FY2013 revenue-line reclassification and operating-metric definition changes.** Read the Q2 2013 basis note, P229 (Q2-FY2013_notes-02-basis-of-presentation-and-recently-issued.txt) p.8, and the RPD clarification, P291 (releases/OTHER-2013-08-01-a.txt) p.1. Question: why was Other revenue reclassified in Q2 2013, and were prior-period metrics recast when RPD was redefined?
25. **T25 (P4), FY2011 and Q4 FY2012 year-end receivables up faster than revenue, allowance rate down, and the Brazil allowance correction.** Read the FY2011 receivables note, P9 (AR-FY2011_financial-statements.txt) p.90, and the revision paragraph in P68 p.83. Question: do Donlen (64.0) and fleet-disposal receivables account for the FY2011 receivables growth, and how much of the Brazil correction was allowance rather than asset charges?

Could not run: consensus.csv is not attached. No consensus figure appears in this output, so no Tier 5 label is needed.

Other E2 computations [calc, C2]: FY2012 revenue less Dollar Thrifty's 43 days (170.6, CS E9) is 8,850.2 against the reaffirmed 8,900 to 9,000; FY2013 adjusted pre-tax income with the 11.6 nine-month correction added back is 1,164.8 against the lowered low end of 1,200.

E2 findings. FY2011: raised twice, then beat the top of the last range on four of five metrics; the Q2 to Q4 remainder beat the top of the remainder implied by the April range on all four additive metrics. FY2012: raised once, then landed inside the range on four metrics. FY2013: held through 29 Jul 2013, lowered on 26 Sep 2013, then missed the low end of the lowered range on all five metrics, with the Q4 shortfall largest on Corporate EBITDA (398.2 against an implied 474.5 to 544.5) [calc, C2]. The FY2014 adjusted pre-tax income range is 16.7% of its midpoint wide, against 4.9% to 8.5% for every earlier range in Table E2-f [calc, C2]. The release states: "We intend to close the guidance range as the year unfolds" [P295 p.6]. Rows: F5-E2-01 (T1), F5-E2-02 (T1), F5-E2-03 (T18).

E3 findings. Both pay metrics are adjusted measures, and the categories they exclude are the ones the earlier steps flagged as recurring (T2). The 2011 performance-unit payout sat 0.5 above a 1,389 target on figures later revised; the API payout factors fall by 7.1 points (2011) and 4.4 points (2012) on the revised API [calc, C3]. The 2011 API target of 525.2 is 0.2 above the low end of the initial FY2011 guidance [calc, C3]. The claw-back policy is triggered by a "restatement" [P297 p.50]; the FY2013 corrections were presented as a revision with no Item 4.02 filing [CS E24; P299]. Rows: F5-E3-01 (T2), F5-E3-02 (T4), F5-E3-03 (T18).

### Checklist E4, turnover and controls (from the context sheet)

- Auditor: PricewaterhouseCoopers LLP for FY2011 to FY2013; no Item 4.01 report from 1 Jan 2011 to 31 Mar 2014; unqualified opinions and effective internal control in all three years; the FY2013 report does not mention the revision [CS section 5].
- Finance leadership: CFO Elyse Douglas resigned effective 1 Oct 2013 with an interim successor; Thomas C. Kennedy appointed CFO effective 9 Dec 2013; CD&R and Carlyle nominees resigned 15 Aug 2013 [CS E20].
- Systems: Oracle general ledger, payables and part of fixed assets implemented in Q3 2013 [CS E19].
- Controls: no material weakness or significant deficiency disclosed; the HVF II Series 2013-B requirement was "unknowingly not met" at 31 Dec 2013 [CS E23]; out-of-period errors identified in Q4 2013 [CS E24]; an earlier set of errors identified in Q3 2011 [CS E4].
- The company is a US SEC registrant with auditor attestation, so the Canadian non-SEC-issuer point does not apply [CS section 1].
- No new row: these items are carried by F1-E4-1, F1-E4-2 and F1-C16-1 (thread T6).

For FY2011 and FY2012 the differences are rounding: the release rounds to 0.1 while those 10-Ks are in thousands. For FY2013, operating cash flow differs by 0.1 between two documents that are both in millions (3,589.7 against 3,589.6; FY2012 as revised 2,709.8 against 2,709.7); the cause is not stated and the amount is recorded only.The FY2012 segment reconciliation prints 450.5 [P48 p.120] against 450.6 in the release and in the FY2013 10-K's "as previously reported" column [P66 line 407], a rounding difference on 450.545 [P37 line 104; calc, C4].

Descriptions that differ: (1) the FY2013 error total, 47.9 in the release against 46.3 in the 10-K and the slides (F5-E6-01); (2) the Q4 FY2011 release calls the Q3 2011 corrections "adjustments" where the 10-K says "errors" (F5-E6-02). The release's Corporate EBITDA, the long-term pay metric, has no amount in any 10-K text file [calc, C4]; the 10-K describes it only in the stock compensation note [P73 p.125]. The FY2012 comparatives in the FY2013 release are the revised ones: adjusted pre-tax income 892.3 against 901.5 as first reported, Corporate EBITDA 1,626.4 against 1,635.6 [calc, C4].

Proxy = (revenue - direct operating - fleet depreciation and lease charges) / revenue, because there is no gross margin line. | P1 IS_Q, IS_A, EXTRA (Direct operating; Depreciation of revenue earning equipment and lease charges), BS_Q, BS_A; Inventory table | Fleet depreciation fell from depreciation-rate changes (car rental -130.6 in 2012) [inf: context sheet E22]; in the paired periods DIO rose by 1.4 days or less, on inventory of 84.0 to 128.3 across the series [calc]; Dollar Thrifty added inventory in Q4 FY2012 [inf: context sheet E9]. | If rate changes do not account for the margin gain, cost could be held on the balance sheet; inventory is too small to hold material cost here, so the place to test that reading is fleet carrying value (F3, F4) [inf: P1 BS_Q]. | E22, E9; E24 moved vendor incentives between SG&A and fleet depreciation, so P1 2012 cost lines are pre-revision (W4) | Depreciation-rate change effects by quarter; the pack has annual amounts only [P45, p. 113; P74, p. 126] | P4 |  |

Triggers (a) and (b) | P1, IS_A, BS_A, CF_A; P16, p. 125; P45, p. 113; P74, p. 126 | Fleet growth and acquisitions (Donlen lease vehicles with contracted residuals, Dollar Thrifty) raise gross fleet and purchases; strong 2011 to 2012 residuals and longer holding periods justify lower rates; on the revised FY2012 base the FY2013 rate (0.1449) is level with FY2012 as revised (0.1448) [calc] | Each year's estimate change moved depreciation down while 2013 disposal results turned to losses, so reported fleet cost may run below eventual realized cost and the gap would surface later | E2, E9, E16 (fleet acquired); E22 (rate changes); E24 | Fleet mix (program versus non-program, Donlen share) and average holding period by year: not found in Notes 2 and 8 or MD&A depreciation sections searched | P2 |  |

Q3 release: ""The Company continues to be affected by the carryover effect of lower fleet efficiency and wholesale losses due to the need to sell vehicles ahead of its planned rotation schedule."" [P293 p.5]","P285 p.4 line 111; P293 p.5 lines 107, 109; P295 lines 488, 530, 553, 652; C2","Guidance is a forecast. F3-C2-01,T2,C2 Recurring adjustments labelled non-recurring or one-time,Q1 FY2011 to Q4 FY2013,"Restructuring adjusted out in 16 of 16 quarters and acquisition related costs in 15 of 16 (Q1 FY2010 to Q4 FY2013); restructuring 49.1 (FY2012) to 100.2 (FY2013) [calc]; both listed each quarter under ""EXTRAORDINARY, UNUSUAL OR NON-RECURRING ITEMS""; definition ""certain one-time charges"" in releases and the filed 10-K; 10-K expects relocation spending ""over the next two years"" while ""Relocation costs"" are adjusted out","P271 to P295, INCOME MEASUREMENTS and Corporate EBITDA tables; P62, pp. 50 and 65; P77, pp. 134 to 135","The non-recurring wording is the credit-agreement definition of Corporate EBITDA (""As defined in the credit agreements for the senior credit facilities""), not management's description; adjusted pre-tax income is the ASC 280 segment measure; restructuring is disclosed every quarter as a multi-year programme (""incremental costs incurred directly supporting our business transformation initiatives"")","Charges present every quarter and doubling in FY2013 are presented as one-time or non-recurring, so adjusted pre-tax income may overstate the recurring run-rate by those amounts; in filed documents Item 10(e) bars the non-recurring label for charges that occurred in the prior two years or are reasonably likely in the next two","E9 (integration), E10; restructuring and relocation are not on the context sheet","Management's basis for the one-time label in the filed MD&A (P62, p. 50 note (c)); Item 10(e)(1)(ii)(B) text not in pack",P2,

## 6. For the analyst

Add a verdict and a short note to every thread (and, where a row differs from its thread, to that row):

- **Explained:** the benign reading holds.
- **Open:** settling it needs more than the pack contains.
- **Of concern:** the adverse reading is supported by the evidence cited.
- **Dismissed:** the row is wrong; give the reason.

Then attach the verdicted `ledger-merged.csv` to F6.

```bash
grep -il "guidance\|outlook" pack/releases/*.txt
```

C1: `c1_load.py`

```python
# C1: load the four ledgers, count rows, validate columns, IDs, locators and readings
import csv, os, collections
RUN = r"C:\Users\aaron\OneDrive\Desktop\Master\WireSift Research\Events\CFA Vancouver Forensic\tests\hertz-fy2013\runs\v0.2-claude-code"
COLS = ["ID","Item","Period","Evidence","Locator","Benign reading","Adverse reading","Context","Resolving disclosure","Priority","Verdict"]
rows, malformed = [], []
for f in ["ledger-F1.csv","ledger-F2.csv","ledger-F3.csv","ledger-F4.csv"]:
    with open(os.path.join(RUN,f), encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
        r = csv.reader(fh)
        header = next(r)
        assert header == COLS, (f, header)
        n = 0
        for line in r:
            if not line: continue
            n += 1
            rec = dict(zip(COLS, line)) if len(line)==len(COLS) else None
            if rec is None:
                malformed.append((f, line[:1], f"{len(line)} fields")); continue
            probs = [c for c in ["ID","Item","Period","Evidence","Locator","Benign reading","Adverse reading","Context","Resolving disclosure","Priority"] if not rec[c].strip()]
            if rec["Priority"] not in ("P1","P2","P3","P4"): probs.append("priority value")
            if rec["Verdict"].strip(): probs.append("verdict not empty")
            if probs: malformed.append((f, rec["ID"], probs)); continue
            rec["_file"] = f
            rows.append(rec)
        print(f, "data rows:", n)
ids = collections.Counter(r["ID"] for r in rows)
dups = [i for i,c in ids.items() if c>1]
print("valid rows:", len(rows), "malformed:", malformed, "duplicate IDs:", dups)
print("priority by step:")
for f in ["ledger-F1.csv","ledger-F2.csv","ledger-F3.csv","ledger-F4.csv"]:
    print(" ", f, collections.Counter(r["Priority"] for r in rows if r["_file"]==f))
print("priority total:", collections.Counter(r["Priority"] for r in rows))
# context sheet row count
with open(os.path.join(RUN,"context-sheet.md"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
    cs = fh.read().splitlines()
print("context-sheet.md lines:", len(cs), "event rows:", sum(1 for l in cs if l.startswith("| E") and "|" in l[3:]))
```

C2: `c2_guidance.py`

```python
# C2: E2 guidance. Every input figure is read from the release line cited and asserted present in that line.
import re, os
PACK = r"C:\Users\aaron\OneDrive\Desktop\Master\WireSift Research\Events\CFA Vancouver Forensic\tests\hertz-fy2013\pack"
PID = {"ER-Q4-FY2010":"P269","ER-Q1-FY2011":"P271","ER-Q2-FY2011":"P273","ER-Q3-FY2011":"P275","ER-Q4-FY2011":"P277",
       "ER-Q1-FY2012":"P279","ER-Q2-FY2012":"P281","ER-Q3-FY2012":"P283","ER-Q4-FY2012":"P285","ER-Q1-FY2013":"P287",
       "ER-Q2-FY2013":"P289","ER-Q3-FY2013":"P293","ER-Q4-FY2013":"P295"}
_cache = {}
def lines(f):
    if f not in _cache:
        with open(os.path.join(PACK, "releases", f + ".txt"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            _cache[f] = fh.read().splitlines()
    return _cache[f]
def page(f, n):
    p = "(no marker)"
    for l in lines(f)[:n]:
        m = re.match(r"\[p\. ([^\]]+)\]", l)
        if m: p = m.group(1)
    return p
def v(f, n, txt):
    l = lines(f)[n - 1]
    assert txt in l, (f, n, txt, l[:200])
    return float(txt.replace(",", "").replace("$", "").replace("(", "-").replace(")", ""))
def loc(f, n): return f"{PID[f]}, p. {page(f, n)}, line {n}"

M = ["Revenue", "Corporate EBITDA", "Adj pre-tax income", "Adj net income", "Adj diluted EPS"]
G = {}
G["FY2011 initial (22 Feb 2011)"] = dict(src=("ER-Q4-FY2010", 94), r=[(7950, 8100), (1265, 1305), (525, 565), (330, 355), None])
G["FY2011 raise 1 (26 Apr 2011)"] = dict(src=("ER-Q1-FY2011", 63), r=[(8100, 8200), (1320, 1360), (595, 625), (375, 395), (0.85, 0.90)])
G["FY2011 raise 2 (2 Aug 2011)"] = dict(src=("ER-Q2-FY2011", 74), r=[(8150, 8250), (1360, 1395), (635, 670), (401, 424), (0.91, 0.96)])
```

```python
# C3: E3 compensation. Targets and results copied from the proxies (asserted against the cited line);
# payout curves recomputed with the interpolation rules the proxies state; revised API from the FY2013 10-K segment note.
import os, re
PACK = r"C:\Users\aaron\OneDrive\Desktop\Master\WireSift Research\Events\CFA Vancouver Forensic\tests\hertz-fy2013\pack"
def L(rel):
    with open(os.path.join(PACK, rel), encoding="utf-8") as fh: return fh.read().splitlines()
P297, P298, P77 = L(r"proxy\PROXY-2012.txt"), L(r"proxy\PROXY-2013.txt"), L("AR-FY2013_notes-11-segment-information.txt")
P295 = L(r"releases\ER-Q4-FY2013.txt")
def num(s): return float(s.replace(",", "").replace("$", "").replace("(", "-").replace(")", ""))
def cell(lines, n, idx):
    parts = [p.strip() for p in lines[n-1].split("|")]
    return num(parts[idx].replace(" million", ""))
def page(lines, n):
    p = None
    for l in lines[:n]:
        m = re.match(r"\[p\. ([^\]]+)\]", l)
        if m: p = m.group(1)
    return p

# --- EICP 2011 (P297 lines 271-275) and 2012 (P298 lines 227-231): API column index 1, revenue index 2
api11 = dict(th=cell(P297,271,1), tg=cell(P297,272,1), hi=cell(P297,273,1), act=cell(P297,274,1))
rev11 = dict(th=cell(P297,271,2), tg=cell(P297,272,2), hi=cell(P297,273,2), act=cell(P297,274,2))
api12 = dict(th=cell(P298,227,1), tg=cell(P298,228,1), hi=cell(P298,229,1), act=cell(P298,230,1))
rev12 = dict(th=cell(P298,227,2), tg=cell(P298,228,2), hi=cell(P298,229,2), act=cell(P298,230,2))
print("API 2011", api11, "p.", page(P297,274)); print("API 2012", api12, "p.", page(P298,230))

def eicp_factor(x, d):
    # proxy rule: threshold 60%, target 100%, high 160%, linear between; above high, slope is half the threshold-to-high slope
    th, tg, hi = d["th"], d["tg"], d["hi"]
    if x < th: return 0.0
    if x <= tg: return 60 + (x - th) / (tg - th) * 40
    if x <= hi: return 100 + (x - tg) / (hi - tg) * 60
    return 160 + (x - hi) * 0.5 * (160 - 60) / (hi - th)

# revised API from P77 (FY2013 10-K): total reportable segments + other reconciling items
seg_line = [i for i, l in enumerate(P77, 1) if l.startswith("Total reportable segments |") and "1,581.7" in l][0]
oth_line = [i for i, l in enumerate(P77, 1) if l.startswith("Other reconciling items(1) |") and "(428.5)" in l][0]
seg = [num(x) for x in P77[seg_line-1].split("|")[1:]]; oth = [num(x) for x in P77[oth_line-1].split("|")[1:]]
api_rev = {2013: round(seg[0]+oth[0],1), 2012: round(seg[1]+oth[1],1), 2011: round(seg[2]+oth[2],1)}
print("Revised API (P77 lines", seg_line, oth_line, "p.", page(P77, seg_line), "):", api_rev)
rev_rev = {2012: 9024.9, 2011: 8299.3}   # P77 line 127 revenue totals
assert "$10,771.9 | $9,024.9 | $8,299.3" in P77[126]

for yr, d, rv, revd, revrev in [(2011, api11, api_rev[2011], rev11, rev_rev[2011]), (2012, api12, api_rev[2012], rev12, rev_rev[2012])]:
    fo, fr = eicp_factor(d["act"], d), eicp_factor(rv, d)
    ro, rr = eicp_factor(revd["act"], revd), eicp_factor(revrev, revd)
    print(f"{yr} API payout: as reported {d['act']} -> {fo:.1f}% ; revised {rv} -> {fr:.1f}% ; change {fr-fo:+.1f} pp ; API change {rv-d['act']:+.1f}")
    print(f"{yr} revenue payout: as reported {revd['act']} -> {ro:.1f}% ; revised {revrev} -> {rr:.1f}% ; change {rr-ro:+.1f} pp")
    print(f"{yr} API actual / target {d['act']/d['tg']*100:.1f}% ; above high performance level by {d['act']-d['hi']:+.1f}")
# 2012 total modifier check against proxy (API 40, revenue 20, EVA 40 at the stated 114.3%)
tm = 0.4*eicp_factor(api12["act"], api12) + 0.2*eicp_factor(rev12["act"], rev12) + 0.4*114.3
tm_rev = 0.4*eicp_factor(api_rev[2012], api12) + 0.2*eicp_factor(rev_rev[2012], rev12) + 0.4*114.3
print(f"2012 total modifier recomputed {tm:.1f}% (proxy 118.7%); with revised API and revenue, EVA held at 114.3%: {tm_rev:.1f}%")
tm11 = 0.4*eicp_factor(api11["act"], api11) + 0.2*eicp_factor(rev11["act"], rev11) + 0.4*153.3
tm11_rev = 0.4*eicp_factor(api_rev[2011], api11) + 0.2*eicp_factor(rev_rev[2011], rev11) + 0.4*153.3
print(f"2011 total modifier recomputed {tm11:.1f}% (proxy 160.8%); with revised API and revenue, EVA held at 153.3%: {tm11_rev:.1f}%")

# --- PSUs: Corporate EBITDA
def psu(x, th, l80, tg, mx):
    if x < th: return 0.0
    if x <= l80: return 50 + (x-th)/(l80-th)*30
    if x <= tg: return 80 + (x-l80)/(tg-l80)*20
    if x <= mx: return 100 + (x-tg)/(mx-tg)*50
    return 150.0
p11 = [cell(P297, n, 1) for n in (397,398,399,400)]
p1112 = [cell(P298, n, 2) for n in (467,468,469,470)]
p12 = [cell(P298, n, 1) for n in (418,419,420,421)]
p1213 = [cell(P298, n, 2) for n in (418,419,420,421)]
ce11, ce12 = 1389.5, 1635.6
assert "$1,389.5 million" in P297[403] and "$1,635.6 million" in P298[424]
ce13 = num([x for x in P295[651].split("|")][6]); ce12_rev = num([x for x in P295[651].split("|")][12])
print("Corporate EBITDA FY2013 and FY2012 revised from P295 line 652:", ce13, ce12_rev)
print(f"2011 PSU: actual {ce11} vs target {p11[2]} -> margin {ce11-p11[2]:+.1f} ({(ce11-p11[2])/p11[2]*100:+.3f}% of target); payout {psu(ce11,*p11):.2f}% (proxy 100.1%)")
print(f"2011 PSU target {p11[2]} vs FY2011 guidance range 1,360-1,395 (2 Aug 2011); 80% level (business plan) {p11[1]} vs initial guidance low end 1,265: diff {p11[1]-1265:+.0f}")
print(f"2011 API target {api11['tg']} vs initial FY2011 adj pre-tax guidance low end 525: diff {api11['tg']-525:+.1f}; threshold {api11['th']}")
print(f"2012 API target {api12['tg']} vs initial FY2012 guidance 790-860 (midpoint 825): diff to midpoint {api12['tg']-825:+.1f} (target adjusted for Dollar Thrifty and Advantage per P298 p.28)")
print(f"2011-2012 PSU: {ce11}+{ce12}={ce11+ce12:.1f} -> payout {psu(ce11+ce12,*p1112):.1f}% (proxy 138.8%)")
print(f"2012 PSU: {ce12} -> {psu(ce12,*p12):.1f}% (proxy 95.1%); on revised 2012 {ce12_rev} -> {psu(ce12_rev,*p12):.1f}%")
s, s_rev = ce12+ce13, ce12_rev+ce13
print(f"2012-2013 PSU combined (targets as set, before any 2013 adjustment): {s:.1f} vs target {p1213[2]} -> {s-p1213[2]:+.1f}, payout {psu(s,*p1213):.1f}% ; with revised 2012: {s_rev:.1f} -> {psu(s_rev,*p1213):.1f}%")
print("pages: P297 EICP", page(P297,274), "PSU", page(P297,400), "clawback", page(P297,534), "| P298 EICP", page(P298,230), "PSU12", page(P298,421), "PSU1112", page(P298,470), "2013 EICP", page(P298,372), "clawback", page(P298,552))
```

```python
# C4: E6 differential disclosure. Q4 release headline figures against the annual filing for the same year.
# Release figures in USD millions; FY2011/FY2012 10-K statements in USD thousands (converted /1000); FY2013 10-K in millions.
import os, re
import openpyxl
PACK = r"C:\Users\aaron\OneDrive\Desktop\Master\WireSift Research\Events\CFA Vancouver Forensic\tests\hertz-fy2013\pack"
def L(rel):
    with open(os.path.join(PACK, rel), encoding="utf-8") as fh: return fh.read().splitlines()
def num(s): return float(s.strip().replace(",", "").replace("$", "").replace("(", "-").replace(")", ""))
USED = []
def grab(lines, n, must, idx):
    # search from the cited line for the first line starting with the label (window of 25 lines); record the line actually used
    cands = [j for j in range(max(1, n-25), min(len(lines), n+25)+1) if must in lines[j-1] and "|" in lines[j-1]]
    assert cands, (n, must)
    j = min(cands, key=lambda x: abs(x-n))
    USED.append((must, j)); return num(lines[j-1].split("|")[idx])
R = {y: L(rf"releases\ER-Q4-FY{y}.txt") for y in (2010, 2011, 2012, 2013)}
K = {2011: L("AR-FY2011_financial-statements.txt"), 2012: L("AR-FY2012_financial-statements.txt"), 2013: L("AR-FY2013_financial-statements.txt")}
SEG = {2011: L("AR-FY2011_notes-10-segment-information.txt"), 2012: L("AR-FY2012_notes-11-segment-information.txt"), 2013: L("AR-FY2013_notes-11-segment-information.txt")}
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(os.path.join(PACK, "statements.xlsx"), data_only=True)
def p1(sheet, label, col):
    ws = wb[sheet]; hdr = [c.value for c in ws[1]]
    for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, values_only=True):
        if row[0] == label: return row[hdr.index(col)]

rows = []
def cmp(year, item, rel, rel_loc, ann, ann_loc):
    used = [u[1] for u in USED[-3:]]
    rel_loc = re.sub(r"line \d+", "line %d" % USED[-2][1] if not ann_loc.startswith("P1") else "line %d" % USED[-1][1], rel_loc) if "segments" not in ann_loc and "lines" not in ann_loc else rel_loc
    if not ann_loc.startswith("P1") and "lines" not in ann_loc: ann_loc = re.sub(r"line \d+", "line %d" % USED[-1][1], ann_loc)
    d = round(rel - ann, 3)
    rows.append((year, item, rel, rel_loc, ann, ann_loc, d))

# FY2010: FY2010 10-K is not a pack text file; P1 FY2010 is that 10-K as originally reported (manifest)
r = R[2010]
cmp(2010, "Revenue", grab(r,186,"Total revenues",1), "P269 line 186", p1("IS_A","Revenue","FY2010"), "P1 IS_A FY2010")
cmp(2010, "Pre-tax income", grab(r,194,"Loss before income taxes",1), "P269 line 194", p1("IS_A","Pre-tax income","FY2010"), "P1 IS_A FY2010")
cmp(2010, "Diluted EPS", grab(r,204,"Diluted",1), "P269 line 204", p1("IS_A","Diluted EPS","FY2010"), "P1 IS_A FY2010")
cmp(2010, "Total assets", grab(r,393,"Total assets",1), "P269 line 393", p1("BS_A","Total assets","FY2010"), "P1 BS_A FY2010")
cmp(2010, "Cash", grab(r,391,"Cash and cash equivalents",1), "P269 line 391", p1("BS_A","Cash and equivalents","FY2010"), "P1 BS_A FY2010")
cmp(2010, "Operating cash flow", grab(r,782,"Net cash provided by operating activities",3), "P269 line 782", p1("CF_A","Cash from operations","FY2010"), "P1 CF_A FY2010")

# FY2011
r, k = R[2011], K[2011]
cmp(2011, "Revenue", grab(r,188,"Total revenues",1), "P277 line 188", grab(k,97,"Total revenues",1)/1000, "P9 line 97")
cmp(2011, "Pre-tax income", grab(r,202,"before income taxes",1), "P277 line 202", grab(k,106,"before income taxes",1)/1000, "P9 line 106")
cmp(2011, "Net income (incl. NCI)", grab(r,204,"Net income (loss) |",1), "P277 line 204", grab(k,108,"Net income (loss) |",1)/1000, "P9 line 108")
cmp(2011, "Net income attributable", grab(r,206,"common stockholders",1), "P277 line 206", grab(k,110,"common stockholders",1)/1000, "P9 line 110")
cmp(2011, "Diluted EPS", grab(r,212,"Diluted",1), "P277 line 212", grab(k,116,"Diluted",1), "P9 line 116")
cmp(2011, "Total assets", grab(r,374,"Total assets",1), "P277 line 374", grab(k,59,"Total assets",1)/1000, "P9 line 59")
cmp(2011, "Cash", grab(r,372,"Cash and cash equivalents",1), "P277 line 372", grab(k,40,"Cash and cash equivalents",1)/1000, "P9 line 40")
cmp(2011, "Operating cash flow", grab(r,604,"Net cash provided by operating activities",3), "P277 line 604", grab(k,249,"Net cash provided by operating activities",1)/1000, "P9 line 249")
```

```python
byid = {r["ID"]: r for r in rows}
assigned = collections.Counter(m for t in TH for m in t["members"])
assert all(c == 1 for c in assigned.values()), [k for k, c in assigned.items() if c > 1]
assert set(assigned) == set(byid), (set(byid) - set(assigned), set(assigned) - set(byid))

def pnum(p): return int(p[1])
for t in TH:
    pr = [pnum(byid[m]["Priority"]) for m in t["members"]]
    steps = sorted({m.split("-")[0] for m in t["members"]})
    t["steps"] = steps
    base = min(pr)
    rule = "highest member priority"
    if len(steps) >= 2 and not t["explained"] and base > 2:
        base = 2; rule = "members from two or more steps, not explained by the context sheet: raised to P2"
    t["prio"] = base; t["rule"] = rule
    t["p1count"] = sum(1 for p in pr if p == 1)
# rank: priority, then most recent period, then number of steps, then number of P1 members, then member count
order = sorted(range(len(TH)), key=lambda i: (TH[i]["prio"], -TH[i]["latest"], -len(TH[i]["steps"]), -TH[i]["p1count"], -len(TH[i]["members"]), i))
for rank, i in enumerate(order, 1):
    TH[i]["tid"] = f"T{rank}"
tid_of = {m: t["tid"] for t in TH for m in t["members"]}
TH_sorted = [TH[i] for i in order]

# ---------- write merged CSV ----------
MCOLS = ["ID","Thread"] + COLS[1:]
merged = []
for r in rows:
    rr = dict(r); rr["Thread"] = tid_of[r["ID"]]; rr["Verdict"] = ""
    merged.append(rr)
merged.sort(key=lambda r: (int(r["Thread"][1:]), r["ID"]))
with open(os.path.join(RUN, "ledger-merged.csv"), "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
    w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=MCOLS, quoting=csv.QUOTE_MINIMAL); w.writeheader()
    for r in merged: w.writerow({k: r[k] for k in MCOLS})

# ---------- checks ----------
# the excluded words are read from rule 1 of the prompt file rather than typed here
PROMPT = r"C:\Users\aaron\OneDrive\Desktop\Master\WireSift Research\Events\CFA Vancouver Forensic\tools\prompts\F5-ledger.md"
rule1 = [l for l in open(PROMPT, encoding="utf-8") if "do not appear in your output" in l][0]
banned = [w.lower() for w in re.findall(r'"([^"]+)"', rule1.split("The words")[1].split("do not appear")[0])]
banned = [b[:-3] if b.endswith("ion") else b for b in banned]   # stem the one noun so verb forms are caught too
print("excluded words checked:", len(banned))
print("input rows:", n_in, "F5 rows:", len(F5), "merged rows:", len(merged))
print("row priority counts:", dict(sorted(collections.Counter(r["Priority"] for r in merged).items())))
print("thread priority counts:", dict(sorted(collections.Counter(f"P{t['prio']}" for t in TH).items())))
print("verdict empty in all rows:", all(r["Verdict"] == "" for r in merged))
for r in merged:
    txt = " ".join(r[c] for c in MCOLS).lower()
    for b in banned:
        if b in txt: print("banned-term hit:", r["ID"], b)
    if "\u2014" in " ".join(r[c] for c in MCOLS): print("em-dash in row:", r["ID"])
for r in F5:
    for c in COLS:
        if "\u2014" in r[c]: print("EM DASH IN F5 ROW", r["ID"], c)
print()
for t in TH_sorted:
    print(t["tid"], f"P{t['prio']}", t["latest"], "steps=" + ",".join(t["steps"]), "n=" + str(len(t["members"])), "|", t["rule"], "|", t["name"])
json.dump(dict(threads=TH_sorted, F5=F5), open(os.path.join(OUT, "threads.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)
```

TJ = json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "threads.json"), encoding="utf-8")); TH = TJ["threads"]
COUNT = {"c": 0, "k": 0}
def fmt(x, nd=1):
    if isinstance(x, str): return x
    if abs(x) < 10 and nd == 2: return f"{x:,.2f}"
    return f"{x:,.{nd}f}"
def C(x, nd=1): COUNT["c"] += 1; return fmt(x, nd)
def K(x, nd=1, sign=False):
    COUNT["k"] += 1
    s = fmt(x, nd)
    return ("+" + s) if (sign and x > 0) else s
def md_row(cells): return "| " + " | ".join(str(c).replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ") for c in cells) + " |"
